## Locator · 屬性

**id**　76f8918b993f3f5568-30

**english**　Locator

**chapter**　
- Python
- 地理編碼模塊
- 類

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/geocoding/locator-class.html#properties

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/geocoding/locator-class.html

**parameter**　subaddressSummaryWithBaseAddress

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- subaddressSummaryWithBaseAddress （讀取和寫入）
- 指定在輸入基礎地址后是否返回某地址處子地址單元的匯總。 子單元的摘要可以是參考資料中基于屬性的子單元值的范圍或者地址的子單元數，后者適用于參考資料是多種單元類型的組合或多個子地址元素映射到定位器中的情況。 只有表示基礎地址的圖徵顯示在定位器中時，在鍵入基礎地址時才可以看到子單元的摘要。 有關如何使用此屬性的詳細信息，請參閱 提高地理編碼質量的提示 。 對于地理編碼服務，此屬性是只讀的。 要在已發布的定位器中更改此屬性，請在本地進行修改，然后再次發布定位器。 注: 此屬性僅設置為運行時屬性。 除非您使用 updateLocator 方法，否則該屬性不會保存至當前會話之外的定位器。
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/geocoding/locator-class.html#properties

## Locator · geocode(searchText, forStorage, {preferredSearchLocation}, {countryCode}, {categories}, {locationType}, {outFields}, {maxResults}, {minScore}, {outputLanguageCode}, {preferredLabelValues}, {searchExtent}, {matchNarrative}, {searchWithin}, {start}, {num}, {matchID}, {comprehensiveZoneMatch})

**id**　76f8918b993f3f5568-37

**english**　Locator

**chapter**　
- Python
- 地理編碼模塊
- 類

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/geocoding/locator-class.html#method-geocode

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/geocoding/locator-class.html

**parameter**　forStorage

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- forStorage
- Specifies whether the results will be stored. When set to False, the results can be temporarily displayed on a map. If you store the results—in a database, for example—set this argument to True. This argument only applies to geocoding requests made with ArcGIS World Geocoding Service. For all other locators, it is ignored. 注: Applications are contractually prohibited from storing the results of geocoding operations using ArcGIS World Geocoding Service unless they make the request by passing this argument with a value of True. For a locator using ArcGIS World Geocoding Service to be valid, you must sign in to ArcGIS Online with a valid organizational account. ArcGIS Online service credits are deducted from the organizational account for each geocode transaction.
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/geocoding/locator-class.html#method-geocode

- 說明

**values**　
- preferredSearchLocation （可選）
- The origin point that will be used to prefer or boost geocoding candidates based on their proximity to the location. Candidates near the location are prioritized relative to those farther away.

- 地理編碼模塊

**parameter**　countryCode

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- countryCode （可選）
- A comma-delimited string of three-character country codes that limits geocoding results to the set of specified countries, which will improve the accuracy of geocoding in most cases. When no country is specified, geocoding with suggestions is performed using all countries supported by the locator. If you are using ArcGIS World Geocoding Service, see the list of supported countries.
- String

**source_language**　zh-cn

- 地理編碼模塊

**parameter**　locationType

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- locationType （可選）
- Specifies the preferred output location that will be used for PointAddress matches. If the preferred location does not exist in the data, the default location will be returned instead. This argument only affects the geometry, not the attribute values. rooftop—An address location such as rooftop location, parcel centroid, or front door will be used. street—A location close to the side of the street that can be used for vehicle routing will be used. (默認值為 rooftop)
- String

**source_language**　zh-cn

- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- outFields （可選）
- The list of fields that will be returned as part of the attributes in the result. This argument supports a single field name or a comma-delimited string of field names (with no spaces). To return the default output fields, this argument does not need to be passed. Use an asterisk ("*") to return all available output fields with the result. The output fields are described in What's included in the geocoded results.
- String

- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- preferredLabelValues （可選）
- Configures the output fields returned in a response from the locator by specifying the address component values that should be included in the output fields. This argument supports a single value or a comma-delimited string of values as input. For more information about how this argument works, see Additional tuning options. Possible values include postalCity, localCity, matchedCity, primaryStreet, and matchedStreet. 注: Only one value for city labeling and one value for street labeling can be included.
- String

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/geocoding/locator-class.html#method-geocode

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/geocoding/locator-class.html

**parameter**　matchNarrative

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- matchNarrative （可選）
- Specifies whether information about how a geocoding result was obtained will be returned. When this argument is set to True, the return value will describe how each portion of an input string in a geocode request was processed, or classified, by the locator. This information is included as an additional JSON object named match-narrative. For example, if the string is 380 New York Street, Redlands, the match-narrative object will describe how each part of the input was matched. In the match-narrative object, each part of the input search string is represented with a subobject, the order of which corresponds to that of the input parts. For example, with an address of 380 New York Street, Redlands, each subobject will include the following keys: field—The address component that the input part was matched to. This key value would have a value of 'House Number'. input—The part of the input string that is being described. This key would have a value of '380'. datum—The value in the locator reference data that the input part matched to. This key would have a value of '380'. narrative-code—A code that describes how the input part was matched. This key would have a value of 'cm'. narrative-desc—A detailed description of how the input part was matched. This key would have a value of 'classified+matched'. For a complete list of the match narrative codes and descriptions, see Match narrative code descriptions. (默認值為 False)
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/geocoding/locator-class.html#method-geocode

## Locator · geocode(searchText, forStorage, {preferredSearchLocation}, {countryCode}, {categories}, {locationType}, {outFields}, {maxResults}, {minScore}, {outputLanguageCode}, {preferredLabelValues}, {searchExtent}, {matchNarrative}, {searchWithin}, {start}, {num}, {matchID}, {comprehensiveZoneMatch})

**id**　76f8918b993f3f5568-49

**english**　Locator

**chapter**　
- Python
- 地理編碼模塊
- 類

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/geocoding/locator-class.html#method-geocode

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/geocoding/locator-class.html

**parameter**　searchWithin

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- searchWithin （可選）
- Specifies the feature types that are returned for a collection of places that are related to a geocoded location. For example, this argument can be used to return all addresses that exist within a postal code or all businesses located at a particular address. Collections can be returned for geocoded matches with Addr_type values PointAddress and PostalExt only. This argument supports a single value or a comma-delimited string of values as input, for example, searchWithin="POI,Subaddress". For more information about how this argument works, see Return associated features or the REST API documentation. Possible values include PointAddress, Subaddress, and POI. This argument supports pagination. Because there can be hundreds or thousands of places that exist at or within a geocoded location, this argument is used with the start and num arguments to break up large collections into manageable result sets, or pages, of 50 or fewer candidates. This is accomplished by passing consecutive requests to the locator with different start values. To support pagination, the JSON response includes the following keys at the end of the response when a collection is returned for a searchWithin request: total—The total count of candidates in the collection, including the geocoded object start—The value that was passed in the request for the start argument num—The number of candidates in the page nextStart—The value that should be used for the start argument in the subsequent geocode request If this argument is used with the ArcGIS World Geocoding Service, the request must also include a valid token. 注: This argument is not supported for composite geocode services. 注: The first object in the response (candidate 1) is always the geocoded object: the place that was searched for. What follows are the features that are associated with the geocoded object and correspond to the feature types specified.
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/geocoding/locator-class.html#method-geocode

- The ID of an address or place. This value can be obtained from the result and used at later time. In most cases, the value for a particular address will be constant across releases of the ArcGIS World Geocoding Service and ArcGIS StreetMap Premium locators; however, for custom locators, the MatchID field value will be as consistent as the data used to build the locator with the Create Locator tool. **parameter**　forStorage

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- forStorage
- Specifies whether the results will be stored. The default value is False, which indicates the results won't be stored, but they can be temporarily displayed on a map. If you store the results, in a database, for example, set this parameter to True. This parameter only applies to geocoding requests made with ArcGIS World Geocoding Service. For all other locators, the parameter is ignored. 注: Applications are contractually prohibited from storing the results of geocoding operations using ArcGIS World Geocoding Service unless they make the request by passing the forStorage parameter with a value of True. For a locator using ArcGIS World Geocoding Service to be valid, you must sign in to ArcGIS Online with a valid organizational account. ArcGIS Online service credits are deducted from the organizational account for each geocode transaction that includes the forStorage parameter with a value of True. (默認值為 False)
- Boolean

## Locator · 返回值

**english**　Locator

- Python
- 地理編碼模塊
- 類

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/geocoding/locator-class.html#method-return-value-reverseGeocode

**text**　import arcpy

# Create a new Locator object from a locator on disk
locator_path = r"C:\Locators\Atlanta.loc"
locator = arcpy.geocoding.Locator(locator_path)

# Create input PointGeometry object
spatial_reference = arcpy.SpatialReference(4326)
point = arcpy.Point(-84.374, 33.797)
point_geometry = arcpy.PointGeometry(point, spatial_reference)

# Reverse geocode to find the nearest address or place to the point
reverse_geocode_result = locator.reverseGeocode(point_geometry, False)

# Create a new Locator object from the ArcGIS World Geocoding Service
locator_path = "https://geocode.arcgis.com/arcgis/rest/services/World/GeocodeServer/ArcGIS World Geocoding Service"
locator = arcpy.geocoding.Locator(locator_path)

# Create input PointGeometry object
spatial_reference = arcpy.SpatialReference(4326)
point = arcpy.Point(-84.374, 33.797)
point_geometry = arcpy.PointGeometry(point, spatial_reference)

# Reverse geocode to find the nearest address or place to the point
geocoding_candidates = locator.reverseGeocode(point_geometry, True)

- A comma-delimited string of three-character country codes that limits suggestion results to the set of specified countries, which will improve the accuracy of suggestions in most cases. - String

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- forStorage
- Specifies whether the results will be stored. The default value is False, which indicates the results won't be stored, but they can be temporarily displayed on a map. If you store the results, in a database, for example, set this parameter to True. This parameter only applies to geocoding requests made with ArcGIS World Geocoding Service. For all other locators, the parameter is ignored. 注: Applications are contractually prohibited from storing the results of geocoding operations using ArcGIS World Geocoding Service unless they make the request by passing the forStorage parameter with a value of True. For a locator using ArcGIS World Geocoding Service to be valid, you must sign in to ArcGIS Online with a valid organizational account. ArcGIS Online service credits are deducted from the organizational account for each geocode transaction that stores results. (默認值為 False)
- Boolean

**english**　Locator

- 地理編碼模塊

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/geocoding/locator-class.html#method-return-value-geocodeWithSuggest

**text**　import arcpy

# Create a new Locator object from a locator on disk
locator_path = r"C:\Locators\Atlanta.loc"
locator = arcpy.geocoding.Locator(locator_path)

# Generate suggestions from partial user input
suggestion_candidates = locator.suggest("1670 w peach")

# Geocode using one of the suggestions generated as the output of the suggest
# operation
geocoding_candidates = locator.geocodeWithSuggest(suggestion_candidates[0], False)

- 類

**text**　import arcpy

# Create a new Locator object from the ArcGIS World Geocoding Service
locator_path = "https://geocode.arcgis.com/arcgis/rest/services/World/GeocodeServer/ArcGIS World Geocoding Service"
locator = arcpy.geocoding.Locator(locator_path)

# Generate suggestions from partial user input
suggestion_candidates = locator.suggest("1670 w peach")

# Geocode using one of the suggestions generated as the output of the suggest
# operation
geocoding_candidates = locator.geocodeWithSuggest(suggestion_candidates[0], True)

## getDefinition · 討論

**id**　77046f3384929a72f1-2

**english**　PictureElement

**chapter**　
- Python
- 制圖模塊
- 類

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/pictureelement-class.html#discussion

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/pictureelement-class.html

**text**　PictureElement 對象表示已插入頁面布局的網格或圖像。 Layout 對象上的 listElements 方法將返回頁面布局元素對象的 Python 列表。 然后需要迭代列表中的每個項目或指定一個索引編號以引用特定的頁面元素對象。 要僅返回 PictureElement 對象的列表，應對 element_type 參數使用 PICTURE_ELEMENT 常量。 還可以使用 wildcard 對基于元素 name 的搜索進行進一步優化。
PictureElement 對象具有 sourceImage 屬性，可用于讀取原始源或指定新圖片源位置。 如果您打算將圖片替換為大小和橫縱比不同的圖片，則使用表示您希望所有其他圖片在頁面布局上占據的整個區域的高度和寬度創作原始圖片。 如果圖片已使用 sourceImage 屬性替換并且具有不同的橫縱比，則它將使用最長尺寸占據原數圖片區域。 已替換圖片不能大于原數創作的尺寸。 您還想要設置錨點位置，以便所有新圖片相對于該位置進行適應。 如果您不想傾斜圖片，確保將 保留橫縱比 選項設置為 True 。
圖片始終存儲在工程中。 sourceImage 屬性會在插入圖片時顯示原始源位置，但是可以從磁盤中移除文件，圖片仍會在布局中顯示。
可通過 ArcGISProject 對象上的 createPictureElement 方法使用頁面單位將圖片添加到 Layout 對象，或使用地圖單位將圖形圖層添加到 Map 對象。 可使用 點 或矩形 面 幾何創建圖片。 新建圖片的默認錨點位置為左上方，該座標表示 X 和 Y 元素位置。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/mapping/pictureelement-class.html#discussion

## getDefinition · 注:

**id**　77046f3384929a72f1-3

**english**　PictureElement

**chapter**　
- Python
- 制圖模塊
- 類

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/pictureelement-class.html#discussion

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/pictureelement-class.html

**text**　可使用 Python CIM 訪問 修改布局中的其他圖片屬性，但是這不適用于圖形圖層中的圖片元素。 這是因為所有圖片資料保留在二進制引用中，以優化性能。 圖形圖層還具有硬限制 4000 個元素或大小限制 10MB。 這意味著無法插入大于 10MB 的單張圖片或超過 10 MB 的任意數量圖片。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/mapping/pictureelement-class.html#discussion

**text**　以下腳本將在目錄結構中搜索工程文件。 對于找到的每個工程，該腳本將循環遍歷所有布局以查找圖片元素。 如果找到了 oldSourcePath ，則會將其替換為 newSourcePath 并打印進行了更改的工程和布局。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/mapping/pictureelement-class.html#method-setDefinition

## getDefinition · 代碼示例

**id**　77046f3384929a72f1-26

**english**　PictureElement

**chapter**　
- Python
- 制圖模塊
- 類

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/pictureelement-class.html#method-setDefinition

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/pictureelement-class.html

**text**　import arcpy, os

rootFolder = r'C:\Projects'
oldSourcePath = r'C:\Projects\OldLogo.png'
newSourcePath = r'C:\Projects\NewLogo.png'

for (rootFolder, dirs, files) in os.walk(rootFolder):
  for file in files:
    if file.endswith('.aprx'):
      p = arcpy.mp.ArcGISProject(os.path.join(rootFolder,file))
      print(os.path.join(rootFolder,file))
      for lyt in p.listLayouts():
        for pic in lyt.listElements('picture_element'):
          if pic.sourceImage == oldSourcePath:
            pic.sourceImage = newSourcePath
            print(f'Found OldLogo: /n  Project: {file} /n  :Layout: {lyt.name}')
      #p.save()  #Test before permenantly replacing

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/mapping/pictureelement-class.html#method-setDefinition

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/pictureelement-class.html#method-setDefinition

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/pictureelement-class.html

**text**　def MakeRec_UL(ulx, uly, w, h):
    xyRecList = [[ulx, uly], [ulx+w, uly], [ulx+w,uly-h], [ulx,uly-h], [ulx,uly]]
    array = arcpy.Array([arcpy.Point(*coords) for coords in xyRecList])
    rec = arcpy.Polygon(array)
    return rec

p = arcpy.mp.ArcGISProject('current')
lyt = p.listLayouts('Layout')[0]

picPath1 = r'C:\Projects\Fenway.jpg'
picPath2 = r'C:\Projects\Chowdah.png'

pic1 = p.createPictureElement(lyt, MakeRec_UL(1,7.5,8.67,6.5), picPath1, 'Fenway')
pic2 = p.createPictureElement(lyt, MakeRec_UL(5.8,7.34,4.2,6.43), picPath2, 'Chowdah')

newGroup = p.createGroupElement(lyt, [pic1, pic2], 'Favorite Pets')

**content_version**　3.7

## getDefinition · 代碼示例

**id**　77046f3384929a72f1-30

**english**　PictureElement

**chapter**

- 制圖模塊
- 類

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/pictureelement-class.html#method-setDefinition

**text**　p = arcpy.mp.ArcGISProject('current')
m = p.listMaps('Map')[0]  #USA Contiguous Albers Equal Area Conic USGS_1
ptLyr = m.listLayers('*Point')[0]
graLyr = m.createGraphicsLayer()
m.moveLayer(ptLyr, graLyr, 'AFTER')

picPath = r'C:\Projects\Chowdah.png'

for row in arcpy.da.SearchCursor(ptLyr, ['SHAPE@XY']):
    x, y = row[0]
    centeredPt = arcpy.Point(x-50000, y+75000) #1/2 width and height
    pic = p.createPictureElement(graLyr, centeredPt, picPath, 'Chowdah')
    pic.elementWidth = 100000   #map units meters
    pic.elementHieght = 150000  #map units meters

## getDefinition · 代碼示例

**id**　77046f3384929a72f1-32

**english**　PictureElement

**chapter**　
- Python
- 制圖模塊
- 類

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/pictureelement-class.html#method-setDefinition

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/pictureelement-class.html

**text**　p = arcpy.mp.ArcGISProject('current')
lyt = p.listLayouts('Layout')[0]

picPath = r'C:\Projects\Fenway.jpg'

picElm = p.createPictureElement(lyt, arcpy.Point(1,5), picPath, 'Fenway')
picElm.elementWidth = 4
picElm.elementHeight = 3

#Create AltText using Python CIM Access
lyt_cim = lyt.getDefinition('V3')
for elm in lyt_cim.elements:
    if elm.name == 'Fenway Pic':
        altTxt = arcpy.cim.CreateCIMObjectFromClassName("CIMStringMap", "V3")
        altTxt.key = "AltText"
        altTxt.value = "Picture of Fenway, a Golden Retriever, staring at sushi."
        elm.customProperties.append(altTxt)
lyt.setDefinition(lyt_cim)

**content_version**　3.7

## GEMI · 討論

**id**　772cc9e09889fd4171-2

**english**　GEMI

**chapter**　
- Python
- 影像分析模塊
- 函數
- 波段索引

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/gemi.html#discussion

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/gemi.html

**text**　“全球環境監測指數”(GEMI) 方法是通過衛星影像進行全球環境監測的非線性植被指數。 該指數與 NDVI 類似，但對大氣影響的敏感度較低。 它受裸土影響，因此，不建議在植被稀疏或中度茂密的區域使用。
\(GEMI \space = \space eta \space * \space (1 \space - \space 0.25 \space * \space eta) \space - \space ((Red \space - \space 0.125)/(1 \space - \space Red))\)
其中，
\(eta \space = \space (2 \space * \space (NIR ^{2} \space - \space Red ^{2} ) \space + \space 1.5 \space * \space NIR \space + \space 0.5 \space * \space Red)/(NIR \space + \space Red \space + \space 0.5)\)
有關其他多波段網格索引的信息，請參見 Band Arithmetic 網格函數。
網格對象所引用的網格資料集是臨時性的。 要將其設置為永久，可以調用網格對象的 save 方法。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/image-analyst/gemi.html#discussion

## updateColorizer · 討論

**id**　774103930d25f68268-2

**english**　Symbology

**chapter**　
- Python
- 制圖模塊
- 類

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/symbology-class.html#discussion

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/symbology-class.html

**text**　典型的工作流是參考圖層的 symbology ，進行更改，然后將更改應用回圖層。
重要的是，應首先確保圖層支持渲染器或著色器并在對其進行更新之前了解其類型。 為此，可使用內置 hasattr 函數。
arcpy.mp 中當前支持的圖徵圖層渲染器如下： SimpleRenderer 、 GraduatedColorsRenderer 、 GraduatedSymbolsRenderer 、 UnclassedColorsRenderer 和 UniqueValueRenderer 。 支持以下網格著色器： RasterClassifyColorizer 、 RasterStretchColorizer 和 RasterUniqueValueColorizer 。
支持的渲染器和著色器僅顯示一組基本屬性。 可以使用 CIM 訪問其他屬性。 Layer 類具有 getSymbologyDefinition 和 setSymbologyDefinition 方法。 這些方法可用于更新不受支持的 arcpy.mp 渲染器或著色器，方法為進行直接的 CIM 更改或交換出從 Map 或 LayerFile 中的另一個圖層引用的符號系統。 下面的第三個和第四個代碼示例演示了這些工作流。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/mapping/symbology-class.html#discussion

## updateColorizer · 代碼示例

**id**　774103930d25f68268-10

**english**　Symbology

**chapter**　
- Python
- 制圖模塊
- 類

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/symbology-class.html#method-updateRenderer

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/symbology-class.html

**text**　以下腳本會遍歷每個圖徵圖層并使用 hasattr 函數測試其 symbology 是否支持 renderer 屬性。 接下來，如果渲染器 type 是 SimpleRenderer ，則腳本將會使用 updateRenderer 方法將其更改為 GraduatedColorsRenderer ，然后將其 breakCount 值設置為 6。最后，它會將符號系統重新應用于圖層，然后再保存結果。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/mapping/symbology-class.html#method-updateRenderer

## updateColorizer · 代碼示例

**id**　774103930d25f68268-11

**english**　Symbology

**chapter**　
- Python
- 制圖模塊
- 類

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/symbology-class.html#method-updateRenderer

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/symbology-class.html

**text**　import arcpy, os, sys
relpath = os.path.dirname(sys.argv[0])

p = arcpy.mp.ArcGISProject(relpath + r'\\Symbol.aprx')
m = p.listMaps('Map')[0]

for lyr in m.listLayers():
  if lyr.isFeatureLayer:
    sym = lyr.symbology
    if hasattr(sym, 'renderer'):
      if sym.renderer.type == 'SimpleRenderer':
        sym.updateRenderer('GraduatedColorsRenderer')
        sym.renderer.breakCount = 6

        lyr.symbology = sym

p.saveACopy(relpath + r"\\SavedOutput.aprx")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/mapping/symbology-class.html#method-updateRenderer

## updateColorizer · 代碼示例

**id**　774103930d25f68268-12

**english**　Symbology

**chapter**　
- Python
- 制圖模塊
- 類

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/symbology-class.html#method-updateRenderer

**text**　以下腳本首先測試圖層的符號系統是否支持 colorizer 屬性，然后確認渲染器是否為 RasterClassifyColorizer 。 接下來，將更改 classificationField 和 breakCount 。 最后，將 ColorRamp 更改為名為 青色到紫色 的色帶。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/mapping/symbology-class.html#method-updateRenderer

## updateColorizer · 代碼示例

**id**　774103930d25f68268-13

**english**　Symbology

**chapter**　
- Python
- 制圖模塊
- 類

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/symbology-class.html#method-updateRenderer

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/symbology-class.html

**text**　import arcpy
import os
import sys

relpath = os.path.dirname(sys.argv[0])

p = arcpy.mp.ArcGISProject(relpath + r'\\RasterClassify.aprx')
m = p.listMaps('Map')[0]
l = m.listLayers('Raster1')[0]
sym = l.symbology

if hasattr(sym, 'colorizer'):
    if sym.colorizer.type == 'RasterClassifyColorizer':
        sym.colorizer.classificationField = 'Value'
        sym.colorizer.breakCount = 7
        sym.colorizer.colorRamp = p.listColorRamps('Cyan to Purple')[0]
        l.symbology = sym

p.saveACopy(relpath + r'\\SavedOutput.aprx')

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/mapping/symbology-class.html#method-updateRenderer

## updateColorizer · 代碼示例

**id**　774103930d25f68268-14

**english**　Symbology

**chapter**　
- Python
- 制圖模塊
- 類

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/symbology-class.html#method-updateRenderer

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/symbology-class.html

**text**　以下腳本演示了如何使用 Python CIM 訪問來修改未在 arcpy.mp API 中顯示的其他符號系統設置。 該腳本將為面符號的輪廓符號圖層添加虛線效果。 該腳本先獲取圖層的 CIM 定義。 隨后，獲取該圖層的第一個符號圖層，即輪廓符號。 如果不存在圖層效果，該腳本將創建一個效果，然后修改虛線效果。 最后，將 CIM 更改內容推送回該圖層。 有關 CIM 的詳細信息，請參閱 Python CIM 訪問 幫助主題。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/mapping/symbology-class.html#method-updateRenderer

## updateColorizer · 代碼示例

**id**　774103930d25f68268-15

**english**　Symbology

**chapter**　
- Python
- 制圖模塊
- 類

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/symbology-class.html#method-updateRenderer

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/symbology-class.html

**text**　p = arcpy.mp.ArcGISProject('current')
m = p.listMaps('Symbology')[0]
l = m.listLayers('States_SingleSymbol')[0]

l_cim = l.getDefinition('V2')         #Get the Layer's CIM definition

#Symbol Level 1 (Solid Stroke)
symLvl1 = l_cim.renderer.symbol.symbol.symbolLayers[0]

#If a dashed effect does not exist, create one
if len(symLvl1.effects) == 0:
  newDash = arcpy.cim.CreateCIMObjectFromClassName('CIMGeometricEffectDashes', 'V2')
  newDash.dashTemplate = [5, 5]
  symLvl1.effects = [newDash]
#If a dashed effects does exist, modify it
else:
  dash = symLvl1.effects[0]
  dash.dashTemplate = [10, 10]

l.setDefinition(l_cim)                #Set the Layer's CIM definition

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/mapping/symbology-class.html#method-updateRenderer

## updateColorizer · 代碼示例

**id**　774103930d25f68268-17

**english**　Symbology

**chapter**　
- Python
- 制圖模塊
- 類

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/symbology-class.html#method-updateRenderer

**text**　import os
p = arcpy.mp.ArcGISProject('current')
m = p.listMaps('Renderers')[0]

relpath = p.homeFolder

#Reference the two layers
l1 = m.listLayers('Counties_SingleSymbol')[0]
l2 = arcpy.mp.LayerFile(os.path.join(relpath, "LYRXs", "Counties_PieChart_MalesFemales.lyrx")).listLayers()[0]

#Set single symbol to Pie chart (from LYRX)
l1.setSymbologyDefinition(l2.getSymbologyDefinition('V3'))

**text**　CrossValidationResult 類除了包含附加只讀屬性外，與 Result 類相似。有關詳細幫助信息，請參閱 交叉驗證 工具。
對于 反距離權重法 、 全局多項式插值法 、 徑向基函數插值法 、 含障礙的擴散插值法 和 含障礙的核插值法 ，只提供平均誤差和均方根誤差結果。
90% 區間、95% 區間和平均 CRPS 僅適用于 經驗貝葉斯克里金法 和 EBK 回歸預測工具 模型。

- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 地統計分析

**text**　# Name: CrossValidation_Example_02.py
# Description: Perform cross validation on an input geostatistical layer.
# Requirements: Geostatistical Analyst extension

# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Set environment settings
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/gapyexamples/data"

# Set local variables
inLayer = "C:/gapyexamples/data/kriging.lyr"

# Run CrossValidation
cvResult = arcpy.ga.CrossValidation(inLayer)
print("Root Mean Square error = " + str(cvResult.rootMeanSquare))

## Describe 對象屬性 · 代碼示例

**id**　77c3d3975c3a776824-15

**english**　Describe object properties

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 函數
- 描述資料
- Describe 屬性

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/describe-object-properties.html#code-sample

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/describe-object-properties.html

**text**　import arcpy

# Create a Describe object
desc = arcpy.Describe("C:/Data/chesapeake.gdb")

# Print some Describe Object properties
if hasattr(desc, "name"):
    print("Name:        " + desc.name)
if hasattr(desc, "dataType"):
    print("DataType:    " + desc.dataType)
if hasattr(desc, "catalogPath"):
    print("CatalogPath: " + desc.catalogPath)

# Examine children and print their name and dataType
print("Children:")
for child in desc.children:
    print("\t%s = %s" % (child.name, child.dataType))

## 函數屬性 · 代碼示例

**id**　78a1aa6bb6bee02df8-7

**english**　Function properties

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 函數
- 描述資料
- Describe 屬性
- 公共設施網絡屬性
- 域網絡屬性
- 層
- 更新子網追蹤配置屬性

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/functions.html#code-sample

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/functions.html

**text**　# Import required modules
import arcpy

# Describe functions on a Utility Network
UN = "C:\\Projects\\MyProject\\unowner.sde\\Naperville.UNOWNER.Naperville\\Naperville.UNOWNER.Naperville"
d = arcpy.Describe(UN)

# Domain Network properties
domnets = d.domainNetworks

# For each domain network in the utility network
for dom in domnets:
    print(f"Domain Network Name: {dom.domainNetworkName}")

    # For each tier in the domain network
    for tier in dom.tiers:
        print(f"Tier Name: {tier.name}")

        # Update Subnetwork Trace Configuration Properties
        ust = tier.updateSubnetworkTraceConfiguration

        # Functions Properties
        print(" - Functions Properties - ")
        for f in ust.functions:
            # Try to get these properties if the exist, else, print the empty list
            try:
                print(f"Function Type: {f.functionType}")
                print(f"Function Network Attribute Name: {f.networkAttributeName}")
                print(f"Function Summary Attribute Name: {f.summaryAttributeName} \n")
                # Function Conditions
                print(" - Function Conditions - ")
                for fc in f.conditions:
                    print(f"Name: {fc.name}")
                    print(f"Type: {fc.type}")
                    print(f"Operator: {fc.operator}")
                    print(f"Value: {fc.value}")
                    print(f"CombineUsingOr: {fc.combineUsingOr}")
                    print(f"Is Specific Value: {fc.isSpecificValue} \n")
            except:
                print("Skipped functions properties. \n")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/functions/functions.html#code-sample

## Polyline · 討論

**id**　7925bbde499a6823c8-2

**english**　Polyline

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#discussion

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html

**text**　在許多地理處理工作流中，您可能需要使用座標和幾何信息運行特定操作，但不一定想經歷創建新（臨時）圖徵類、使用光標填充圖徵類、使用圖徵類，然后刪除臨時圖徵類的過程。 可以使用幾何對象替代輸入和輸出，從而使地理處理變得更簡單。 使用 Geometry 、 Multipoint 、 PointGeometry 、 Polygon 或 Polyline 類可以從頭開始創建幾何對象。
在創建幾何對象的過程中，將執行一個簡化過程，以使幾何根據其幾何類型在拓撲上保持一致。 例如，將校正可能自相交的面，或包含錯誤環方向的面。 座標可能會在幾何空間參考的分辨率范圍內移動。
可以使用以下運算符代替幾何方法：

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#discussion

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- length3D （只讀）
- 線狀圖徵的 3D 長度。 該計算將使用 3D 笛卡爾算法。 對于點和多點幾何，長度為零。 對于面幾何，長度將是邊界的 3D 長度。 注意： 該操作不執行水平和垂直座標系之間的單位轉換。 假設 x、y 和 z 座標在同一線性單位中。 注意： 此屬性僅適用于投影資料。
- Double

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#properties

## Polyline · cut(cutter)

**id**　7925bbde499a6823c8-52

**english**　Polyline

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#method-cut

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html

**text**　將該幾何分割到剪切折線的左右兩側。
剪切折線或面時，將在其與剪切折線相交的位置進行分割。 每一塊都被分類為剪切線的左側或右側。 此分類基于剪切線的方向。 目標折線中不與剪切折線相交的部分將作為輸入折線結果右側的部分返回。 如果未對幾何進行剪切，則左側幾何將為空 (None)。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#method-cut

## Polyline · densify(method, distance, {deviation})

**id**　7925bbde499a6823c8-56

**english**　Polyline

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#method-densify

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html

**parameter**　method

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- method
- The method of densification. DISTANCE—Creates a feature that is a piecewise linear approximation of the input. ANGLE—Creates a feature that is a piecewise linear approximation of the input. Vertices are introduced at points where the angle between tangents at those points is the provided angle. GEODESIC—Densifies and reshapes segments between input vertices so that the output segments follow the shortest ground path connecting input vertices.
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#method-densify

## Polyline · densify(method, distance, {deviation})

**id**　7925bbde499a6823c8-57

**english**　Polyline

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#method-densify

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html

**parameter**　distance

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- distance
- The maximum distance between vertices. The actual distance between vertices will usually be less than the maximum distance, as new vertices will be evenly distributed along the original segment. If using a type of DISTANCE or ANGLE, the distance is measured in the units of the geometry's spatial reference. If using a type of GEODESIC, the distance is measured in meters.
- Double

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#method-densify

## Polyline · densify(method, distance, {deviation})

**id**　7925bbde499a6823c8-58

**english**　Polyline

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#method-densify

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html

**parameter**　deviation

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- deviation （可選）
- Densify uses straight lines to approximate curves. You use deviation to control the accuracy of this approximation. The deviation is the maximum distance between the new segment and the original curve. The smaller its value, the more segments will be required to approximate the curve. If using a type of DISTANCE, the deviation is measured in the units of the geometry's spatial reference. If using a type of ANGLE, the deviation is measured in radians. If using a type of GEODESIC, the deviation is not used.
- Double

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#method-densify

**id**　7925bbde499a6823c8-75

**kind**　說明

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html

**text**　使用測量方法返回圖徵的長度。

**content_version**　3.7

## Polyline · getLength({method}, {units})

**id**　7925bbde499a6823c8-76

**english**　Polyline

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#method-getLength

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html

**parameter**　method

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- method （可選）
- The method used to measure length. GEODESIC—The shortest line between any two points on the earth's surface on a spheroid (ellipsoid). One use for a geodesic line is to determine the shortest distance between two cities for an airplane's flight path. This is also known as a great circle line if based on a sphere rather than an ellipsoid. GREAT_ELLIPTIC—The line on a spheroid (ellipsoid) defined by the intersection at the surface by a plane that passes through the center of the spheroid and the start and endpoints of a segment. This is also known as a great circle when a sphere is used. LOXODROME—The line follows a single compass bearing, or azimuth. Great circle routes are often broken into a series of loxodromes, which simplifies navigation. This is also known as a rhumb line. PLANAR—Planar measurements use 2D Cartesian mathematics to calculate lengths and areas. This option is only available when measuring in a projected coordinate system and the 2D plane of that coordinate system will be used as the basis for the measurements. Planar measurements reflect the projection of geographic data onto a 2D surface, and do not account for the curvature of the earth. PRESERVE_SHAPE—This type calculates the area or length of the geometry on the surface of the earth ellipsoid, for geometry defined in a projected or geographic coordinate system. This option preserves the shape of the geometry in its coordinate system. (默認值為 GEODESIC)
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#method-getLength

## Polyline · intersect(other, dimension)

**id**　7925bbde499a6823c8-82

- 幾何

**text**　構造一個幾何，它是兩個輸入幾何的幾何交集。 不同的維數可用于創建不同的 shape 類型。
對于同一 shape 類型的兩個幾何體，其交集為僅包含原始幾何重疊區域的幾何。
為了更快地獲取結果，請在調用 intersect 類之前先測試兩個幾何體是否 disjoint 。

**source_language**　zh-cn

## Polyline · pointFromAngleAndDistance(angle, distance, {method})

**id**　7925bbde499a6823c8-101

**english**　Polyline

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#method-pointFromAngleAndDistance

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html

**parameter**　method

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- method （可選）
- Specifies the method that will be used to measure distance. GEODESIC—The shortest line between any two points on the earth's surface on a spheroid (ellipsoid). One use for a geodesic line is to determine the shortest distance between two cities for an airplane's flight path. This is also known as a great circle line if based on a sphere rather than an ellipsoid. GREAT_ELLIPTIC—The line on a spheroid (ellipsoid) defined by the intersection at the surface by a plane that passes through the center of the spheroid and the start and endpoints of a segment. This is also known as a great circle when a sphere is used. LOXODROME—The line follows a single compass bearing, or azimuth. Great circle routes are often broken into a series of loxodromes, which simplifies navigation. This is also known as a rhumb line. PLANAR—Planar measurements use 2D Cartesian mathematics to calculate lengths and areas. This option is only available when measuring in a projected coordinate system and the 2D plane of that coordinate system will be used as the basis for the measurements. Planar measurements reflect the projection of geographic data onto a 2D surface, and do not account for the curvature of the earth. (默認值為 GEODESIC)
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#method-pointFromAngleAndDistance

## Polyline · positionAlongLine(value, {use_percentage}, {geodesic})

**id**　7925bbde499a6823c8-104

**english**　Polyline

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#method-positionAlongLine

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html

**parameter**　value

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- value
- The distance along the line. The distance will be evaluated based on the geodesic parameter value. If the geodesic parameter value is False, the distance will be evaluated in meters. If the geodesic parameter value is True, the distance will be evaluated in the units of the feature's spatial reference. If the value exceeds the length of the line or precedes the length of the line (a negative value), the behaviour will depend on the geodesic parameter value as follows: If the geodesic parameter value is False, exceeding the length of the line will return the endpoint of the line, and a negative distance value will return the starting point of the line. If the geodesic parameter value is True, exceeding or preceding the length of the line will cause the method to fail.
- Double

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#method-positionAlongLine

## Polyline · projectAs(spatial_reference, {transformation_name})

**id**　7925bbde499a6823c8-108

**english**　Polyline

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#method-projectAs

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html

**text**　將幾何從一個空間參考投影到另一個空間參考。
當幾何對象和目標空間參考沒有公共基準面時，請指定 transformation_name 參數值。 有關詳細信息，請參閱 地理基準面變換 和 ListTransformations 。
當滿足以下條件時，將執行垂直變換：
幾何對象具有 z 座標。
幾何對象的空間參考和 spatial_reference 參數值均具有垂直座標系。
transformation_name 參數值為垂直變換。
當 transformation_name 參數值是垂直變換，并且幾何對象和目標空間參考都沒有垂直座標系時，函數將運算失敗，并顯示 ValueError 異常。
如果幾何對象或 spatial_reference 參數值具有 未知空間參考 ，則輸出幾何的空間參考將是 spatial_reference 參數值的空間參考。 不建議使用未知空間參考進行分析。
projectAs 方法不會修改 m 值（如果存在）。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#method-projectAs

## Polyline · rotate({origin}, {rotation_angle})

**id**　7925bbde499a6823c8-118

**english**　Polyline

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#method-rotate

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html

**text**　將幾何圍繞指定原點旋轉指定角度（以弧度表示）。
旋轉相對于原點應用，以便幾何圍繞原點旋轉。 原點的選擇對于旋轉幾何的位置影響很大。
旋轉不適用于 z 方向。
以下將介紹原點及其對輸出的影響的一些可能的選擇：
原點位于幾何外（不在幾何內或接觸幾何）- 幾何的位置將發生變化，但該幾何與其他以相同方式旋轉的幾何的相對位置保持不變。 位置將圍繞原點旋轉。
原點位于幾何的質心（質心位于幾何內部或接觸幾何）- 幾何將圍繞其質心旋轉。
原點位于幾何的頂點 - 幾何將圍繞所選頂點旋轉。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#method-rotate

- Point

## Polyline · scale({origin}, {sx}, {sy}, {sz})

**id**　7925bbde499a6823c8-122

**english**　Polyline

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#method-scale

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html

**text**　從指定的原點沿 x、y 和 z 軸按指定的因子縮放幾何以創建新幾何。 已變換點（或頂點）的位置由以下提供：
\(( \space (x \space - \space origin_x) \space * \space scale_x \space + \space origin_x, \space (y \space - \space origin_y) \space * \space scale_y \space + \space origin_y, \space (z \space - \space origin_z) \space * \space scale_z \space + \space origin_z \space )\)
相對于原點應用變換，以使原點保持靜止，而幾何圍繞其擴展或收縮。 所生成幾何的擴展或收縮位置和方向受到原點的顯著影響。
以下將介紹原點及其對輸出的影響的一些可能的選擇：
原點位于幾何外（不在幾何內或接觸幾何） - 幾何的大小和位置將發生變化，但幾何將保持與以相同方式縮放的其他幾何的相對位置。 位置將遠離原點擴展或向原點收縮。
原點位于幾何的質心（如果質心位于幾何內部或接觸幾何）- 幾何的大小將發生變化，但幾何的位置將保持錨定在質心。 幾何將圍繞質心增大或收縮。
原點位于幾何的頂點 - 幾何的大小將發生變化，但幾何的位置將保持錨定在所選頂點。 幾何將從所選頂點開始增長或向頂點收縮。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#method-scale

## Polyline · union(other)

**id**　7925bbde499a6823c8-142

**english**　Polyline

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#method-union

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html

**text**　構造一個幾何，該幾何是輸入幾何的并集。
兩個幾何必須具有同一形狀類型。
結果是單個幾何，它是兩個輸入幾何的組合。 任一輸入幾何中的任何內容都將成為結果的一部分。 但是，兩個幾何共有的內容在結果中只出現一次。
輸入幾何的順序對輸出沒有影響。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#method-union

## Polyline · within(second_geometry, {relation})

**id**　7925bbde499a6823c8-145

**english**　Polyline

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#method-within

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html

**text**　指定基礎幾何是否位于比較幾何之內。
within 方法是與 contains 方法相反的運算符。
本圖僅顯示 True 關系。
如果基礎幾何是這些幾何的交集且其內部交集不為空，則基礎幾何位于比較幾何之內。 within 方法是 Clementini 運算符，在空的基礎幾何中除外。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#method-within

## Polyline · 代碼示例

**id**　7925bbde499a6823c8-150

**english**　Polyline

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 類
- 幾何

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#method-return-value-within

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/classes/polyline.html

**text**　import arcpy

# Create a spatial reference from a wkid
spatial_ref = arcpy.SpatialReference(32145)

# A list of features and coordinate pairs
feature_info = [[[1, 2], [2, 4], [3, 7]],
                [[6, 8], [5, 7], [7, 2], [9, 5]]]

# A list that will contain the Polyline objects
features = []

# Create Polyline objects from an array of points
for feature in feature_info:
    array = arcpy.Array([arcpy.Point(*coords) for coords in feature])
    polyline = arcpy.Polyline(array, spatial_ref)
    features.append(polyline)

# Persist a copy of the Polyline objects using CopyFeatures
arcpy.management.CopyFeatures(features, "c:/geometry/polylines.shp")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/classes/polyline.html#method-return-value-within

## 調試 Python 代碼 · 調試 Python 代碼

**id**　79b0eac54cda6d03ce-1

**english**　Debug Python code

**chapter**　
- Python
- 入門
- 調試

**text**　Python 代碼可用于 ArcGIS Pro 的很多部件。 有時需要集成式開發環境 (IDE) 來調試代碼。 使用 Python IDE 創作并解決腳本工具、Python 工具箱源代碼和驗證代碼的問題。
ArcGIS Pro 使用 debugpy 模塊幫助調試程序附加到正確的線程。 debugpy 包是支持使用各種 IDE 調試 Python 代碼的 Python 包。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/get-started/debugging-python-code.html

**id**　79b0eac54cda6d03ce-2

**text**　從 ArcGIS Pro 3.6 及更高版本開始，調試時不再需要 arcpy.SetupDebugger 函數。 如果系統沒有自動檢測到斷點，arcpy.SetupDebugger 函數仍可以在 import 語句后或單獨函數中插入到腳本中。 在完成調試后，移除對 arcpy.SetupDebugger 的所有調用。

**content_version**　3.7

## 調試 Python 代碼 · Microsoft Visual Studio Code

**id**　79b0eac54cda6d03ce-3

**text**　可以使用適用于 Visual Studio Code 的 ArcGIS Pro 調試程序擴展模塊在 Visual Studio Code 中調試以下 ArcGIS Pro 組件：
腳本工具驗證代碼（源，驗證）
Python 工具箱代碼（源，驗證）
要開始在 Visual Studio Code 中調試腳本工具，請點擊鏈接安裝并閱讀文檔“適用于 Visual Studio Code 的 ArcGIS Pro 調試程序擴展模塊”。 要報告該擴展模塊的漏洞或提出增強建議，請在 Esri/arcgispro-vscode-debugger GitHub 資源檔案庫中提交問題工單。
要從 PowerShell 或終端會話安裝擴展模塊，請執行以下命令：code --install-extension esri-arcgis-pro.arcgis-pro-debugger。

**content_version**　3.7

## 調試 Python 代碼 · pdb 模塊

**id**　79b0eac54cda6d03ce-6

**english**　Debug Python code

**chapter**　
- Python
- 入門
- 調試

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/get-started/debugging-python-code.html#3F1

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/get-started/debugging-python-code.html

**text**　pdb 模塊是 Python 標準庫的一部分。 該模塊并非完全開發的 IDE，但可用于交互式源代碼調試。 雖然無法使用 pdb 模塊在 ArcGIS Pro 中進行調試，但您可以使用它來調試獨立腳本，包括腳本工具的源代碼。
pdb 模塊可用于調試正在獨立 Python 腳本中運行的腳本工具源代碼。 使用 breakpoint 函數向腳本工具代碼中添加斷點，然后運行調用該腳本工具的 Python 腳本。 在遇到斷點時，Python 會進入交互模式。 移除調試工作完成時斷點代碼。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/get-started/debugging-python-code.html#3F1

## Mean · 語法

**id**　79f2426ce77707d587-5

**english**　Mean

**chapter**　
- Python
- 影像分析模塊
- 函數
- 統計分析

**kind**　表格

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/mean.html#syntax

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/mean.html

**parameter**　

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 參數
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- extent_type
- The method that will be used to compute the extent of the output when the input rasters have different extents. FirstOf—The output extent will be defined using the extent of the first input raster. LastOf—The output extent will be defined using the extent of the last input raster. IntersectionOf—The output extent will be defined as the intersecting area of the input rasters. UnionOf—The output extent will be defined as the total extent of the input rasters. (默認值為 FirstOf)
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/image-analyst/mean.html#syntax

## Mean · 語法

**id**　79f2426ce77707d587-6

**english**　Mean

**chapter**　
- Python
- 影像分析模塊
- 函數
- 統計分析

**kind**　表格

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/mean.html#syntax

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/mean.html

**parameter**　

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 參數
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- cellsize_type
- The method that will be used to compute the pixel size of the output when the input rasters have different pixel sizes. FirstOf—The output pixel size will be defined using the pixel size of the first input raster. LastOf—The output pixel size will be defined using the pixel size of the last input raster. MaxOf—The output pixel size will be defined using the maximum pixel size of the input rasters. MeanOf—The output pixel size will be defined using the mean of both input rasters. MinOf—The output pixel size will be defined using the minimum pixel size of the input rasters. (默認值為 FirstOf)
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/image-analyst/mean.html#syntax

## Mean · 語法

**id**　79f2426ce77707d587-7

**english**　Mean

**chapter**　
- Python
- 影像分析模塊
- 函數
- 統計分析

**kind**　表格

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/mean.html#syntax

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/mean.html

**parameter**　

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 參數
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- ignore_nodata
- Specifies whether NoData values will be ignored in the calculation. True—The analysis will include all valid pixels in the input rasters and ignore any NoData pixels. False—The analysis will result in NoData if there are any NoData values for the pixels in the input rasters. (默認值為 False)
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/image-analyst/mean.html#syntax

## Mean · 語法

**id**　79f2426ce77707d587-8

**english**　Mean

**chapter**　
- Python
- 影像分析模塊
- 函數
- 統計分析

**kind**　表格

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/mean.html#syntax

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/image-analyst/mean.html

**parameter**　

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 參數
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- process_as_multiband
- Specifies how the bands of the input rasters will be processed. True—Each multiband raster will be processed as a multiband raster. The operation will be performed for each band from one input using the corresponding band number from other inputs. False—Each band from a multiband raster input will be processed separately as a single-band raster. (默認值為 False)
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/image-analyst/mean.html#syntax

## GetSolverProperties · 討論

**id**　7a0854f3261e5ff04a-2

**english**　GetSolverProperties

**chapter**　
- Python
- Network Analyst 模塊
- 舊版本
- 函數

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/network-analyst/getsolverproperties.html#discussion

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/network-analyst/getsolverproperties.html

**text**　當修改網絡分析圖層的分析屬性時， GetSolverProperties 用作主入口點。 它根據網絡分析圖層返回單獨的求解程序屬性對象。 支持以下求解程序屬性對象： RouteSolverProperties 、 ClosestFacilitySolverProperties 、 ServiceAreaSolverProperties 、 ODCostMatrixSolverProperties 、 VehicleRoutingProblemSolverProperties 和 LocationAllocationSolverProperties 。 每個求解程序屬性對象都提供了對特定于某個網絡分析圖層的分析屬性的讀寫訪問權限。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/network-analyst/getsolverproperties.html#discussion

## GetSolverProperties · 舊版本：

**id**　7a0854f3261e5ff04a-8

**english**　GetSolverProperties

**chapter**　
- Python
- Network Analyst 模塊
- 舊版本
- 函數

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/network-analyst/getsolverproperties.html#code-sample

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/network-analyst/getsolverproperties.html

**text**　# Name: GetSolverProperties_01.py
# Description: Generate 3-minute service areas around fire stations at several
#               times of day to compare coverage differences due to varying
#               traffic conditions. Use GetSolverProperties to access the
#               Service Area layer's solver properties object, and update the
#               time of day property prior to each solve.
# Requirements: Network Analyst extension

# Import system modules
import os
import datetime
import arcpy

try:
    # Set environment settings
    output_dir = "C:/Data"
    # The NA layer's data will be saved to the workspace specified here
    arcpy.env.workspace = os.path.join(output_dir, "Output.gdb")
    arcpy.env.overwriteOutput = True

    # Set local variables
    input_gdb = "C:/Data/SanFrancisco.gdb"
    network = os.path.join(input_gdb, "Transportation", "Streets_ND")
    layer_name = "FireStationCoverage"
    out_featureclass = os.path.join(output_dir, "Output.gdb",
                                                        "FireStationCoverage")
    travel_mode = "Driving Time"
    facilities = os.path.join(input_gdb, "Analysis", "FireStations")
    times_of_day = [datetime.datetime(2014, 9, 25, 7, 0, 0),
                    datetime.datetime(2014, 9, 25, 12, 30, 0),
                    datetime.datetime(2014, 9, 25, 17, 30, 0),
                    datetime.datetime(2014, 9, 25, 21, 0, 0)]

    # Create a new service area layer.
    result_object = arcpy.na.MakeServiceAreaAnalysisLayer(network, layer_name,
                                                travel_mode, "FROM_FACILITIES",
                                                [3], polygon_detail="HIGH",
                                                geometry_at_overlaps="OVERLAP")

    # Get the layer object from the result object. The service area layer can
    # now be referenced using the layer object.
    layer_object = result_object.getOutput(0)

    # Get the names of all the sublayers within the service area layer.
    sublayer_names = arcpy.na.GetNAClassNames(layer_object)
    # Stores the layer names that we will use later
    facilities_layer_name = sublayer_names["Facilities"]
    polygons_layer_name = sublayer_names["SAPolygons"]

    # The input data has a field for FireStationID that we want to transfer to
    # our analysis layer. Add the field, and then use field mapping to transfer
    # the values.
    arcpy.na.AddFieldToAnalysisLayer(layer_object, facilities_layer_name,
                                                    "FireStationID", "TEXT")
    field_mappings = arcpy.na.NAClassFieldMappings(layer_object,
                                                    facilities_layer_name)
    field_mappings["FireStationID"].mappedFieldName = "FireStationID"

    # Load the fire stations as facilities.
    arcpy.na.AddLocations(layer_object, facilities_layer_name, facilities,
                            field_mappings, "")

    # Add fields to the output Polygons sublayer for later use.
    arcpy.na.AddFieldToAnalysisLayer(layer_object, polygons_layer_name,
                                        "FireStationID", "TEXT")
    arcpy.na.AddFieldToAnalysisLayer(layer_object, polygons_layer_name,
                                        "TimeOfDay", "TEXT")

    # Get sublayers to work with later
    facilities_sublayer = arcpy.na.GetNASublayer(layer_object, "Facilities")
    polygons_sublayer = arcpy.na.GetNASublayer(layer_object, "SAPolygons")

    # Get the Service Area Layer's solver properties. This can be used to
    # set individual properties later without re-creating the layer.
    solver_properties = arcpy.na.GetSolverProperties(layer_object)

    # Solve the Service Area for each time of day in the time list
    for t in times_of_day:

        print("Calculating service area for time of day: ", t)

        # Use the solver properties to set the time of day for the solve
        solver_properties.timeOfDay = t

        # Solve the service area layer
        arcpy.na.Solve(layer_object)

        # Transfer the FireStationID field from the input Facilities to the
        # output Polygons
        arcpy.management.AddJoin(polygons_sublayer, "FacilityID",
                                        facilities_sublayer, "ObjectID")
        # The joined fields are qualified by the feature class name of the joined
        # table, so determine the feature class names
        field_qualifier_pol = os.path.basename(polygons_sublayer.dataSource)
        target_field_name = "%s.FireStationID" % field_qualifier_pol
        field_qualifier_fac = os.path.basename(facilities_sublayer.dataSource)
        expression = "!%s.FireStationID!" % field_qualifier_fac
        arcpy.management.CalculateField(polygons_sublayer, target_field_name,
                                        expression, "PYTHON")
        arcpy.management.RemoveJoin(polygons_sublayer)

        # Populate the TimeOfDay field in the output feature class
        expression = '"' + str(t) + '"'
        arcpy.management.CalculateField(polygons_sublayer, "TimeOfDay",
                                            expression, "PYTHON")

        # Append the polygons to the output feature class. If this was the first
        # solve, create the feature class.
        if not arcpy.Exists(out_featureclass):
            arcpy.management.CopyFeatures(polygons_sublayer, out_featureclass)
        else:
            arcpy.management.Append(polygons_sublayer, out_featureclass)

    print("Script completed successfully")

except Exception as e:
    # If an error occurred, print line number and error message
    import traceback, sys
    tb = sys.exc_info()[2]
    print("An error occured on line %i" % tb.tb_lineno)
    print(str(e))

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/network-analyst/getsolverproperties.html#code-sample

## 什么是 Spatial Analyst 模塊 · 什么是 Spatial Analyst 模塊

**id**　7a353c44d4d9cc428f-1

**english**　What is the Spatial Analyst module

**chapter**　
- Python
- Spatial Analyst 模塊

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/what-is-the-spatial-analyst-module.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/what-is-the-spatial-analyst-module.html

**text**　需具備 Spatial Analyst 許可方可使用。
Spatial Analyst 模塊 (arcpy.sa) 是一個 Python 模塊，通過 ArcGIS Spatial Analyst 擴展模塊提供的功能分析網格和向量資料。 借助該模塊，可訪問 Spatial Analyst 工具箱中可用的所有地理處理工具以及其他可以自動化網格處理工作流的函數和類。
該模塊包含四個主要組件：類、函數、地理處理函數和運算符。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/spatial-analyst/what-is-the-spatial-analyst-module.html

**text**　除了 arcpy.sa 模塊中的可用類、函數、地理處理函數和運算符之外，也可以使用 Python 腳本中的 ArcPy 或其他 ArcPy 模塊中的函數和類來完成工作流。 Raster ArcPy 類將創建一個可在 Python 腳本中使用的網格對象，并且 arcpy.sa 模塊中提供的大部分處理均需要此網格對象。 NumPyArrayToRaster ArcPy 函數和 RasterToNumPyArray ArcPy 函數對于腳本工作流也非常有用。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/spatial-analyst/what-is-the-spatial-analyst-module.html#857

## addItem · 討論

**id**　7b7cd097f1d9d9c93e-2

**english**　LegendElement

**chapter**　
- Python
- 制圖模塊
- 類

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/legendelement-class.html#discussion

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/legendelement-class.html

**text**　LegendElement 是一種特定類型的 MapSurroundElement 布局元素。 該元素還具有與 MapFrame 對象的關聯，包含特定于圖例的附加屬性，包括對 LegendItem 對象的訪問。 Layout 對象上的 listElements 方法將返回頁面布局元素對象的 Python 列表。 如要僅返回 LegendElement 對象的列表，請為 element_type 參數使用 LEGEND_ELEMENT 常量。 還可以使用 wildcard 值，基于元素的 name 值對搜索進行進一步優化。 為每個頁面布局元素提供唯一名稱，以便輕松地引用元素。
您可以使用 Layout 對象的 createMapSurroundElement 方法，將 map_surround_type 設置為 LEGEND 以創建圖例。可以在創建期間指定現有的 StyleItem 值以控制元素在布局中的顯示方式。
默認情況下，添加到 Map 對象的圖層將自動添加到 LegendElement 對象。 這樣便可以使用空地圖和圖例創建布局，但在將新圖層添加到地圖時動態更新這些項目。 有多個以 'sync' 為開頭的屬性可用于控制如何將項目添加到圖例。 您也可以使用 fittingStrategy 和 columnCount 屬性控制圖例的外觀。
elementPositionX 和 elementPositionY 值取決于元素的錨點位置，該位置在 格式 選項卡上進行設置。 如果希望在添加圖層時 LegendElement 對象向下延伸，請將錨點位置設置為左上角。 您可以使用 setAnchor 方法更改位置。
items 屬性將返回 LegendItem 類列表。您可以單獨對這些類進行修改，以進一步自定義圖例的內容。 LegendElement 類還提供 AddItem 、 MoveItem 和 RemoveItem 方法，以便您管理圖例中的項目。 LegendElement 和 LegendItem 類僅提供對常用屬性的訪問權限。 可以使用 Python CIM 訪問 對內容進行細化訪問。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/mapping/legendelement-class.html#discussion

**parameter**　isOverflowing

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- isOverflowing （只讀）
- 如果圖例中的所有內容均不在所提供幾何的邊界內，則返回 True 。 這種情況下，布局上通常會顯示紅色省略號。 該屬性在 while 循環的上下文中效果很好。 它可以支持更改字體大小、適應策略或元素大小，直到所有內容均能容納。
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/mapping/legendelement-class.html#properties

## addItem · 代碼示例

**id**　7b7cd097f1d9d9c93e-47

**english**　LegendElement

**chapter**　
- Python
- 制圖模塊
- 類

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/legendelement-class.html#method-setDefinition

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/legendelement-class.html

**text**　import arcpy
p = arcpy.mp.ArcGISProject(r'C:\Projects\YosemiteNP\Yosemite_Trails.aprx')

#Create a new legend and set legend properties
lyt = p.listLayouts('Main Attractions')[0]
mf = lyt.listElements('MapFrame_Element', 'Yose*')[0]
legSi = p.listStyleItems('ArcGIS 2D', 'LEGEND', key='*Legend 3*' )[0]
leg = lyt.createMapSurroundElement(arcpy.Point(0.75,3.5), 'LEGEND', mf, legSi, 'New Legend Element')
leg.elementWidth = 7
leg.elementHeight = 3
leg.fittingStrategy = 'AdjustFontSize'
leg.columnCount = 5
leg.title = 'Yosemite National Park'

#Add a layer file but first turn of legend layer synchronization
leg.syncNewLayer = False
lyr = arcpy.mp.LayerFile(r'C:\Projects\YosemiteNP\LayerFiles\Terrain.lyrx')
m = p.listMaps('Yosemite National Park')[0]
m.addLayer(lyr, 'BOTTOM')
leg.syncNewLayer = True
p.saveACopy(r"C:\Projects\YosemiteNP\Yosemite_Trails_OUT.aprx")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/mapping/legendelement-class.html#method-setDefinition

**text**　以下腳本可將 fittingStrategy 設置為 ManualColumns ，然后將 columnCount 設置為 4 。 接下來，腳本會構建一個字典，其中包含圖例項名稱和用于放置圖例項的列。 最后，腳本會遍歷每個圖例項，并基于字典值將其放置在適當的列中。

**content_version**　3.7

## addItem · 代碼示例

**id**　7b7cd097f1d9d9c93e-49

**english**　LegendElement

**chapter**　
- Python
- 制圖模塊
- 類

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/legendelement-class.html#method-setDefinition

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/legendelement-class.html

**text**　import arcpy, os, sys
relpath = os.path.dirname(sys.argv[0])

p = arcpy.mp.ArcGISProject(os.path.join(relpath, 'NewMexicoGeology', 'NewMexicoGeology.aprx'))
lyt = p.listLayouts('Layout')[0]
leg = lyt.listElements('LEGEND_ELEMENT')[0]

#Change Title and fitting strategy to (4) Manual Columns
leg.fittingStrategy = 'ManualColumns'
leg.columnCount = 4

#Dictionary of item name and column pairs
legDict = {'Cretaceous' :    1,
           'Mississippian' : 1,
           'Tertiary' :      2,
           'Silurian' :      2,
           'Ordovician' :    2,
           'Quaternary' :    3,
           'Jurassic' :      3,
           'Devonian' :      3,
           'Permian':        4,
           'Proterozoic' :   4,
           'Other' :         4 }

#Place each legend item into the appropriate column based on name
for legItm in leg.items:
  legItm.column = legDict[legItm.name]

p.saveACopy(os.path.join(relpath, 'NewMexicoGeology', 'NewMexicoGeology_OUT.aprx'))

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/mapping/legendelement-class.html#method-setDefinition

**text**　以下腳本使用 Python CIM 訪問修改圖例的標題符號系統和圖面寬度。 該腳本還使用 CIM 修改唯一值渲染器中每個類的圖面形狀。

**content_version**　3.7

## addItem · 代碼示例

**id**　7b7cd097f1d9d9c93e-51

**english**　LegendElement

**chapter**　
- Python
- 制圖模塊
- 類

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/legendelement-class.html#method-setDefinition

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/legendelement-class.html

**text**　p = arcpy.mp.ArcGISProject('current')
lyt = p.listLayouts('Layout')[0]
leg = lyt.listElements('LEGEND_ELEMENT')[0]

#Use Legend poperties to display and modify title text
leg.showTitle = True
leg.title = "Legend CIM Changes"

#CIM modifications to Title font properties and patch width
lyt_cim = lyt.getDefinition('V3')
for elm in lyt_cim.elements:
  if elm.name == "Legend":
    leg_cim = lyt_cim.elements[1]
    leg_cim.titleSymbol.symbol.height = 30
    leg_cim.titleSymbol.symbol.horizontalAlignment = 'Center'
    leg_cim.titleSymbol.symbol.fontStyleName = 'Bold'
    leg_cim.titleSymbol.symbol.symbol.symbolLayers[0].color.values = [255,0,0,100]
    for itm in reversed(elm.items):       #Done in reversed order
      itm.patchWidth = 50
lyt.setDefinition(lyt_cim)

#CIM modification to layer patch shape (it affects legend items)
m = p.listMaps('Overview Map')[0]
for itm in leg.items:
  lyr = m.listLayers(itm.name)[0]
  if lyr.symbology.renderer.type == 'UniqueValueRenderer':
    lyr_cim = lyr.getDefinition('V3')
    for grp in lyr_cim.renderer.groups:   #Iterate through each group
      for cls in grp.classes:             #Iterate through each class
        cls.patch = "AreaNaturalPoly"     #Called Natural area in app
  lyr.setDefinition(lyr_cim)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/mapping/legendelement-class.html#method-setDefinition

**text**　以下腳本會先將渲染器更改為 UniqueValueRenderer 。 接下來，它將 fields 屬性設置為使用名為 Percent 的單一欄位。 接下來，它會遍歷每個 ItemGroup 中的每個 Item ，并將 RGB 顏色設置為紅色，將 alpha 或不透明度值設置為與各個圖徵的 Percent 值相匹配。 最后，它會對各個值進行標記，將符號系統設置回圖層并保存輸出。

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/mapping/item-class.html

**text**　import arcpy, os, sys
relpath = os.path.dirname(sys.argv[0])

p = arcpy.mp.ArcGISProject(relpath + r'\\UniqueValue.aprx')
m = p.listMaps('Map')[0]
l = m.listLayers('PercentCover*')[0]
sym = l.symbology

sym.updateRenderer('UniqueValueRenderer')
sym.renderer.fields = ['Percent']
for grp in sym.renderer.groups:
    for itm in grp.items:
        transVal = itm.values[0][0] #Grab the first "percent" value in the list of potential values
        itm.symbol.color = {'RGB': [255, 0, 0, int(transVal)]}
        itm.label = str(transVal) + '%'

l.symbology = sym
p.saveACopy(relpath + r'\\SavedOutput.aprx')

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/mapping/item-class.html#properties

**english**　Output Condition properties

**text**　公共設施網絡輸出條件屬性示例（獨立腳本）
以下獨立 Python 腳本可打印某些公共設施網絡屬性的報告。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/functions/output-conditions.html#code-sample

## 輸出條件屬性 · 代碼示例

**id**　7c600bd573f6c38853-9

**english**　Output Condition properties

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 函數
- 描述資料
- Describe 屬性
- 公共設施網絡屬性
- 域網絡屬性
- 層
- 更新子網追蹤配置屬性

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/output-conditions.html#code-sample

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/output-conditions.html

**text**　# Import required modules
import arcpy

# Describe functions on a Utility Network
UN = "C:\\Projects\\MyProject\\unowner.sde\\Naperville.UNOWNER.Naperville\\Naperville.UNOWNER.Naperville"
d = arcpy.Describe(UN)

# Domain Network properties
domnets = d.domainNetworks

# For each domain network in the utility network
for dom in domnets:
    print(f"Domain Network Name: {dom.domainNetworkName}")

    # For each tier in the domain network
    for tier in dom.tiers:
        print(f"Tier Name: {tier.name}")

        # Update Subnetwork Trace Configuration Properties
        ust = tier.updateSubnetworkTraceConfiguration

        # Output Condition Properties
        print(" - Output Condition Properties - ")
        for oc in ust.outputConditions:
            # Try to get these properties if the exist, else, print the empty list
            try:
                print(f"Name: {oc.name}")
                print(f"Type: {oc.type}")
                print(f"Operator: {oc.operator}")
                print(f"Value: {oc.value}")
                print(f"CombineUsingOr: {oc.combineUsingOr}")
                print(f"Is Specific Value: {oc.isSpecificValue} \n")
            except:
                print("Skipped output condition properties. \n")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/functions/output-conditions.html#code-sample

## AspectSlope · 語法

**id**　7c71bb7637c1f0ad51-5

**english**　AspectSlope

**chapter**　
- Python
- Spatial Analyst 模塊

**kind**　表格

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/aspectslope.html#syntax

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/aspectslope.html

**parameter**　

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 參數
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- z_factor
- The z-factor is a scaling factor used to convert the elevation values for the following purposes: Convert the elevation units (such as meters or feet) to the horizontal coordinate units of the dataset, which may be feet, meters, or degrees. Add vertical exaggeration for visual effect. If the x,y units and z units are in the same units of measure, the z-factor should be set to 1. The z-values of the input surface are multiplied by the z-factor when calculating the final output surface. (默認值為 1)
- Double

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/spatial-analyst/aspectslope.html#syntax

## AspectSlope · 代碼示例

**id**　7c71bb7637c1f0ad51-10

**english**　AspectSlope

**chapter**　
- Python
- Spatial Analyst 模塊
- 函數
- 表面分析

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/aspectslope.html#code-sample

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/aspectslope.html

**text**　# Import the system modules
import arcpy
from arcpy.sa import *

# Set the analysis environments
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/arcpyExamples/data"

# Set the local variables
in_raster = "elevation.tif"

# Run the AspectSlope function
out_aspectslope_raster = AspectSlope(in_raster, 3)

# Save the output
out_aspectslope_raster.save("C:/arcpyExamples/outputs/aspectslope.tif")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**text**　在描述圖徵類和表時，圖徵類和表具有一個用于返回 Field 對象列表的 fields 屬性，以及一個用于返回 Index 對象列表的 indexes 屬性。 每個欄位或索引對象都有許多可用于瀏覽對象的屬性。 另外，使用 ListFields 和 ListIndexes 函數也可以創建相同的列表。

**content_version**　3.7

## 使用欄位和索引 · 使用欄位和索引

**id**　7cde60843b12ae8f7c-5

**english**　Using fields and indexes

**chapter**　
- Python
- 入門
- 處理資料

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/get-started/fields-and-indexes.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/get-started/fields-and-indexes.html

**text**　import arcpy
fc = 'D:/St_Johns/data.gdb/roads'
# Get a list of field objects
fields = arcpy.ListFields(fc, 'Flag')
for field in fields:
 # Check the field name, perform a calculation when finding the field 'Flag'
 if field.name == 'Flag':
     # Set the value for the field and exit loop
     arcpy.management.CalculateField(fc, 'Flag', '1')
     break

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/get-started/fields-and-indexes.html

## GEMI · 匯總

**id**　7ceacf444537b82386-1

**english**　GEMI

**chapter**　
- Python
- Spatial Analyst 模塊
- 函數
- 波段索引

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/gemi.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/gemi.html

**text**　計算多波段網格對象的全球環境檢測指數 (GEMI)，并返回具有該指數值的網格對象。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/spatial-analyst/gemi.html#summary

## GEMI · 討論

**id**　7ceacf444537b82386-2

**english**　GEMI

**chapter**　
- Python
- Spatial Analyst 模塊
- 函數
- 波段索引

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/gemi.html#discussion

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/gemi.html

**text**　“全球環境監測指數”(GEMI) 方法是通過衛星影像進行全球環境監測的非線性植被指數。 該指數與 NDVI 類似，但對大氣影響的敏感度較低。 它受裸土影響，因此，不建議在植被稀疏或中度茂密的區域使用。
\(GEMI \space = \space eta \space * \space (1 \space - \space 0.25 \space * \space eta) \space - \space ((Red \space - \space 0.125)/(1 \space - \space Red))\)
其中，
\(eta \space = \space (2 \space * \space (NIR ^{2} \space - \space Red ^{2} ) \space + \space 1.5 \space * \space NIR \space + \space 0.5 \space * \space Red)/(NIR \space + \space Red \space + \space 0.5)\)
有關其他多波段網格索引的信息，請參見 Band Arithmetic 網格函數。
網格對象所引用的網格資料集是臨時性的。 要將其設置為永久，可以調用網格對象的 save 方法。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/spatial-analyst/gemi.html#discussion

## NbrRectangle · 許可：

**id**　7d0d1445bb2abbcd00-3

**english**　NbrRectangle

**chapter**　
- Python
- Spatial Analyst 模塊
- 類
- 鄰域類

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/nbrrectangle-class.html#discussion

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/nbrrectangle-class.html

**text**　如果您擁有 Image Analyst 擴展模塊許可，也可以使用該類，但僅限于 焦點統計 工具。
通過提供以像元或地圖單元為單位的 width 和 height ，指定矩形鄰域。
當在焦點工具中使用矩形時，鄰域范圍內待處理像元相對于鄰域左上角的 x,y 位置可通過以下方程式來確定：
\(x \space = \space (鄰域的寬度 \space + \space 1)/2 \space y \space = \space (鄰域的高度 \space + \space 1)/2\)
如果輸入像元數為偶數，則將使用截斷來計算 x,y 座標。
僅將中心在定義對象內的像元作為矩形鄰域的一部分進行處理。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/spatial-analyst/nbrrectangle-class.html#discussion

## NbrRectangle · 代碼示例

**id**　7d0d1445bb2abbcd00-14

**english**　NbrRectangle

**chapter**　
- Python
- Spatial Analyst 模塊
- 類
- 鄰域類

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/nbrrectangle-class.html#properties

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/spatial-analyst/nbrrectangle-class.html

**text**　# Name: NbrRectangle_Ex_02.py
# Description: Uses the NbrRectangle object to run BlockStatistics tool.
# Requirements: Spatial Analyst extension

# Import system modules
import arcpy
from arcpy import env
from arcpy.sa import *

# Set environment settings
env.workspace = "C:/sapyexamples/data"

# Set local variables
inRaster = "block"

# Create the Neighborhood Object
width = 5
height = 6
myNbrRec = NbrRectangle(width, height, "MAP")

# Run BlockStatistics
outBlkStat =  BlockStatistics(inRaster, myNbrRec, "MINIMUM", "DATA")

# Save the output
outBlkStat.save("C:/sapyexamples/output/blstat_rec3")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/spatial-analyst/nbrrectangle-class.html#properties
